1b1(rgyud): @#//rgya gar skad du/shrI gu h+ya badzra tan tra rA dzA/bod skad du/dpal gsang ba rdo rje rgyud 
1b2(rgyud): kyi rgyal po dpal rdo rje mkha' 'gro la phyag 'tshal lo//dpal ldan 'khor lo sdom pa ru//gsang ba'i 
1b3(rgyud): 'khor lo mi gsung pa//bdag gis zab mo'i don ma rtogs//de phyir khyed kyis bdag la gsungs//
1b4(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//nyon cig lha mo spyan bzang ring //gsang ba'i 'khor lo shin tu 
1b5(rgyud): zab||rgyud rnams kun tu ma gsungs pa//khyod kyis don mdzad pa yis bshad//gsang ba rdo rje rgyud kyi 
1b6(rgyud): rgyal po las gleng bslang ba'i le'u ste dang po'o////lha mos gsol pa bcom ldan 'das//gsang ba 
1b7(rgyud): zhes pa ji ltar lags//de yi 'khor lo ji ltar lags//de yi bdam ji 
1b8(rgyud): ltar lags//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka'astsal pa//gsang ba zhes pa rnam pa bzhi//gsang ba'i pho brang 
2a1(rgyud): @#//
2b1(rgyud): de bzhin bsro bar bya//chu rnams che na chung bar bya//'di 'dra'i b+ha ga gang la yod//bud med 'di la b+ha ga mnga'//de phyir bud med bcom ldan 'das//thabs kyi rdo rje phra sbom dang //ring ba dang ni thung ba 
2b2(rgyud): ste//shes rab ji bzhin thabs la'o//de yi gnas su bzung bar bya//gsang ba rdo rje rgyud kyi rgyal po las gsang ba rtogs pa'i le'u ste gsum pa'o////lha mos gsol pa bcom ldan 'das//e ba~M rnam pa'i 
2b3(rgyud): dbus gang du//bde ba'i khyad par tshol ba la//'di las khyad par med dam ci//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//shin du dben pa'i gnas btsal la//bza' ba bcud can sna tshogs dang //btung ba 
2b4(rgyud): zhim po mang po dang //gur gum la sogs dri zhim dang //shin tu 'jam pa'i stan ting la//rnal 'byor ma yi gnas bcu ru//dri zhim thig le re re bya//rnal 'byor pha yi gnas lnga ru//de bzhin thig le 
2b5(rgyud): lnga yang bya//de nas mkha' gsang byin gyis brlab//e las gru gsum gting ring bsam//de yi nang du pa~M las ni//pad ma 'dab ma brgyad du bsam//de yi dbus su 'khor lo bsam//'khor lo'i dbus 
2b6(rgyud): su re mig dgod//de nas thabs kyi rdo rje yang //hU~M las rdo rje rtse lnga bsam//oM las rdo rje mgo bor bsam//phaT las rdo rje bu ga bsam//de nas bde ba'i stan thog du//sbyor ba rnam pa gsum bya ste//
2b7(rgyud): yan lag thabs dang sbyor ba dngos//'khor lo nyi shu rtsa dgu la//chags pa skye ba'i dri yis byug/bde ba skye ba'i zas zos nas//bde ba'i stan la 'dug nas ni//thabs kyis gnas dgur 'o yang bya//
2b8(rgyud): shes rab kyis kyang gnas lngar bya//thabs kyis shes rab pad ma la//'o byas pad ma rgyas su gzhug/shes rab kyis kyang de bzhin no//chags pa skye ba'i rtags 'byung ste//de nas gcig 
3a1(rgyud): @#//gis gcig 'khyud nas/he ru ka dang phag mor bsam//shes rab rkang pa gnyis brkyang nas//thabs ni thabs ldan gyis bskyod nas//nang rtags phyi ru bstan pa'i phyir//shes rab ma la rtags 
3a2(rgyud): gsum 'byung //lus 'dar 'phar ba sku yi rtags//mi bzod skad 'don ngag gi rtags//shes pa rgyal ba thugs kyi rtags//'di ni gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor ste//'di yis skra dkar gnyer ma spong //nad 
3a3(rgyud): kyi zug rngu thams cad 'joms//'di ni gsang ba'i bcud kyis len//phyi yi dkyil 'khor bsgoms pa min//gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor nyid la gnas//sbyin bsreg la sogs bya ba min//gsang ba'i rdo rje 
3a4(rgyud): rgyud 'di ni//kun gyis thos par mi 'gyur te//glegs bam sor ni bcu gnyis bya//glegs bam gsang ba'i gnas su sba//yongs su smin pa'i rnal 'byor ma//'ga' yis mthong gi gzhan la sba//
3a5(rgyud): gsang ba ston pa'i bla ma ni//sangs rgyas nyid pas btsun pa yin//gzhan yang 'dod pa'i stan thog tu//gsungs pa'i sbyor thabs kun byas nas//lnga bcu nas bzung stong gi bar//rnal 'byor ma kun bde med kyang //
3a6(rgyud): sbyor thabs mkhas pas bskyod byas nas//bcu gnyis ma dang bde ba mnyam//sbyor thabs mi shes sbyor byas pas//rlung mkhris bad kan kun nas skye//shes rab kyis kyang thabs la ste//sbyor thabs mkhas pas shes 
3a7(rgyud): rab ma//thabs kyis rtag tu bsten par bya//sbyor thabs mkhas pa'i rnal 'byor pas//rnal 'byor ma yi gsang la bsten//gsang la bsten pa ji ltar lags//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//ming srid tshul 
3a8(rgyud): lam rje 'bangs tshul//pha spang tshul lam mkhan slob tshul//gzhan gyis mi rig ngo tsha bsrung //dben pa'i gnas su sbyor ba bya//gal te ma bsten dam tshig nyams//tshe 'di nyid la dmyal bar ltung //lha mos 
3b1(rgyud): gsol pa bcom ldan 'das//thabs med rnal 'byor ji ltar lags//bde ba khyad med ji ltar lags//phyag rgya ma bsten ji ltar lags//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//bde ba la gnas 
3b2(rgyud): gsang ba'i don//khyod kyis ngor mdzad nga yis bshad//sbyor thabs mi shes rnal 'byor dang //bstan yang gcig la mi 'dug ste//'dod chags bsam pa yang mi bya//gal te de 'dra bsten gyur na//'jig 
3b3(rgyud): rten phal pa'i 'dod chags te//shi nas mnar med dmyal bar skye//'dod chags spyod pa shal ma ri//lcags kyi sor ni bcu drug pa//yar la 'dzeg cing mar la 'bab//thabs med rnal 'byor 'dra ba 
3b4(rgyud): 'bod//de phyir sbyor thabs mkhas pas bsten//sbyor thabs las ni bde ba skye//de phyir bde ba khyad par 'phags//bde ba khyad med lus zungs nyams//rlung mkhris bad kan kun nas skye//las kyi phyag 
3b5(rgyud): rgya rtag tu bsten//phyag rgya ma bsten dam tshig nyams//phyag rgya bsten pa'i dus su yang //rnal 'byor ming srid brda yis mtshon//gang zhig 'dzub mo ston pa na//'ongs sam zhes ni 'dri ba yin//sor mo 
3b6(rgyud): gnyis ni ston pa dang //mthe bo drang por ston pa dang //de bzhin srin lag drang por ston//rdo rje bo la zhes bya ste//pad ma k+ka ko la zhes bya//de ltar ma byas dam tshig nyams//brda yi bye brag ma lus pa//
3b7(rgyud): sam bu Ta ru shes par bya//lha mos gsol pa bcom ldan 'das//rdo rje pad ma dga' ba'i gnas//de ru bzung ba ji ltar lags//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//de ru gzung ba rnam pa gnyis//rab kyi 
3b8(rgyud): dbugs dang shes pas bzung //dang po btsan thabs sbyor bas bzung //sbyor ba rnam pa bcu ru bzung //gcig ni sbom po khro mos bzung //bzung ba'i rten ni rnam pa bzhi//man ngag can gyis shes par bya//
4a1(rgyud): @#//gal te de ru ma zin na//'khor bar ltung bas rang gis su//ting nge 'dzin gyis ha~M la bsdu//lce yis byang chub sems blangs la//ro ma'i 'khor lo ru ni bstim//de ltar ma byas dam tshig nyams 
4a2(rgyud): lha mos gsol pa bcom ldan 'das//shes rab bskyod thabs 'ba' zhig gam//thabs kyis bskyod pa 'ba' zhig gam//rnal 'byor pha dang rnal 'byor ma//bde ba khyad par med dam ci//bde ba'i ngo bo ji 
4a3(rgyud): ltar lags//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//bskyod pa bud med kyis kyang bskyod//sbyor thabs kyang ni thabs kyis bya//de las bde ba khyad par 'phags//dper na dar dang snal ma bzhin//thabs kyis 
4a4(rgyud): snal ma'i cha tsam myong //shes rab dar gyi sham bu tsam//de phyir rnal 'byor ma'i rgyud//rnal 'byor ma yi 'grub par gsungs//rgyud kyi nang na mchog yin pas//rab gnas sbyin sreg dbang bskur dang //mchod 
4a5(rgyud): dang bzlas dang gtor ma dang //khrus dang bsrung ba la sogs pa//gsang ba 'di las gzhan na med//'bras bu'i nang na 'di mchog ste//ye shes lnga yi spros dang bral//de nas lha mos 'khor dpag tu 
4a6(rgyud): med pa dang /mkha' 'gro ma rnams 'dod chags kyi the tshom yongs su zad nas/bcom ldan 'das la kun gyis 'khyud nas gsang ba'i mchod pas bde ba chen po'i ros gang bar mchod nas mi snang bar 
4a7(rgyud): gyur to//gsang ba'i yang gsang ba gsang ba rdo rje'i rgyud kyi rgyal po/'khor lo sdom pa'i rtog pa rgyu dang 'bras bu'i de kho na bstan pa'i le'u ste/bzhi pa'o////gsang ba rdo rje'i rgyud kyi rgya po zhes bya ba rdzogs s+ho//
4a8(rgyud): ba na D-i ta ra da na badzra dang /lo ts+tsha ba dag slang shAkya ye shes kyis bsgyur te gtan la phab pa'o////rgya gar skad du/shI gu h+ya sarba ts+tshin d+ha ta na tra rA dzA/bod skad du/dpal gsang ba thams cad 
4b1(rgyud): gcod pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po//dpal rdo rje sems dpa' la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus cig na//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang /
4b2(rgyud): thugs rdo rje btsun mo'i b+ha ga la snyoms par zhugs so//de nas byang chub sems dpa' rdo rje snying po la sogs pa'i 'khor rnams kyis g.yon gyi phyogs nas bskor ba lan gsum byas nas phyi nang 
4b3(rgyud): gsang ba'i mchod pas mchod nas 'di skad ces gsol to//kye ho bcom ldan rdo rje 'dzin//gsang ba zhes pa ji ltar lags//de yi gcod pa ji ltar lags//rgyud kyi don ni ji ltar lags//de yi 
4b4(rgyud): rgyal po'ang ji ltar lags//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//gsang ba zhes pa rnam pa bzhi//dang po bum dbang dang 'brel ba'i//bskyed pa'i rim pa'i rnal 'byor te//rten dang rten par bsgom pa ni//snod 
4b5(rgyud): bcud gzung 'dzin rnam rtog rnams//spang phyir nga yis gsungs pa yin//de yi khyad par dgongs pa'i don//dang po stong nyid la sogs pa//rgyud gzhan du ni nga yis gsungs//rten dang rten pa'i bye brag 
4b6(rgyud): la//dang po rten gyi dgongs pa yang //thugs kha'i yi ge hU~M las ni//mar me cig las gnyis kyi tshul//kha dog nag po'i e 'phros pas//gru gsum rgya che gting ring bsam//gru gsum sku gsung thugs yin 
4b7(rgyud): te//dug gsum 'joms pa dgongs pa'i don//logs ni rnam thar gsum yin te//lus ngag yid gsum las byung ba'i//mi dge bcu 'joms dgongs pa'i don//chos kyi sku ni dri med kyis//rang bzhin 
4b8(rgyud): gyis ni kun la khyab//de phyir rgya chen dgongs pa'i don//'phags pa nyan thos rang rgyal dang //byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis kyang //chos sku rtogs pa ma yin pas//gting ring ba ru dgongs pas 
5a1(rgyud): @#//bshad//de nas thugs kha'i hU~M las yang //hU~M 'phros pa la sa rdo rjer gyur//rdo rje gur du gyur pa yang //dang po tshe yi bar chad dang //gnyis pa thar pa'i bar chad de//bgegs ni rnam pa gnyis 
5a2(rgyud): yin te//phyi yi bgegs dang nang gi bgegs//phyi yis tshe yi bar chad de//nang gis thar pa'i bar chad byed//bar chad rnam pa gnyis bsrung ba//rdo rje gur gyi dgongs pa'i don//yaM las byung ba'i gong dang 'dra//dbyibs 
5a3(rgyud): ni zla gam lta bu ste//rnam par rtog pa spong ba dang //mi rtog pa ni skye ba la//dbang du byed pas dbang gis don//ba dan bzhi yi dgongs pa yang //chos rnams dus gsum gnas pa yi//dgongs pa yin par nga yis gsungs//
5a4(rgyud): ra~M las me yis dkyil 'khor ni//zur gsum drag po'i dgongs pa yin//rdo rje rtse gsum sku gsum don//ba~M las chu yi dkyil 'khor te//zlum po spros pa zhi bar gsungs//bum pa chos sku'i yan lag bzhi//
5a5(rgyud): la~M las gser gyi sa gzhi ste//gru bzhi rgyas pa'i rang bzhin te//rdo rje rtse lnga ye shes lnga//de stengs ri rab rtse mo brgyad//rnam thar brgyad kyi dgongs pa yin//snod bcud rnam rtog mi mnga'i rtags//dur khrod brgyad ni 
5a6(rgyud): g.yas dang g.yon//go rims bzhin du dgod par bya//re re'i bye brag rnam rtog yang //bla ma'i zhal las shes par bya//gnyen po'i stobs ni yongs su rgyas//shing rnams rims bzhin rtag tu dgod//rnam rtog mi dge 
5a7(rgyud): bcu'i don//'joms pa'i rtags su phyogs skyong dgod//'dod chags zhe sdang ma rig pa//gdug pa 'joms phyir klu brgyad dgod//thugs rje chen po dang ldan pa//gzugs sku gnyis kyi nga rgyal gyis//lus dang ngag gi sems 
5a8(rgyud): can gyis//don mdzad phyir ni char sprin dgod//nang dang gsang ba de bzhin no//de bzhin mthar thug bzhi pa yang //snyan nas brgyud pa shes par bya//de nas nang du gzhal yas khang //b+h+ru~M las 'khor lo rtsibs brgyad de//
5b1(rgyud): rtsibs brgyad rnam shes brgyad kyis dngos//yongs su gyur pas rnam snang sku//phyi nas ltas pas nang du gsal//de bzhin nang nas phyi ru ste//thams cad gcig tu snang ba'i don//tshon chen sna lnga sku lnga'i don//kha dog lnga ni 
5b2(rgyud): rigs lnga'i don//gru bzhi tshangs pa'i gnas bzhir gsungs//logs bzhi bsdu ba'i dngos po ste//sgo bzhi dran pa nyer bzhag yin//ka ba brgyad ni rnam thar brgyad//gdung bzhi ye shes bzhi yi don//twa ra na la sogs pa 
5b3(rgyud): kun//stobs la sogs pa yin par gsungs//de nas nang du gdan rnams ni//pad ma'i sa bon dbyangs las skyes//'dod chags la sogs nyon mongs kyis//ma gos rtags su pad ma'i gdan//de steng nyi 
5b4(rgyud): ma zla ba ste//thabs dang shes rab gnyis kyi brda//dang po ro ni nga bdag gam//rnam rtog 'joms phyir gsungs pa yin//yan lag bzhi ldan gtso bo'i gdan//'khor la re re gdan du sbyin//gtso bo'i yan lag 
5b5(rgyud): yin pa'i phyir//dgongs pa'i don dang bcas pa yis//rten gyi dkyil 'khor rdzogs pa'o//de nas lha rnams bskyed pa ni//skye ba bzhi yi tshul yin te//skad cig las byung rdzus pa'i tshul//dper na chu gting las ni 
5b6(rgyud): nya//rkyen las skyes pa drod gsher skyes//dper na nyi mas pad ma bzhin//spro zhing bsdu ba sgong skyes tshul//dper na rdo las zangs bzhin no//sbyor ba las byung mngal skyes tshul//yar la sreg cing mar la gso//
5b7(rgyud): dper na sba tshil las ni gzugs//pho brang rnam par snang mdzad de//rjes su bskyed pa he ru ka/dbang bskur bcom ldan mi bskyod pa//bstod pa rin chen 'byung ldan yin//mchod pa don yod grub pa'o//bdud rtsi 
5b8(rgyud): myang ba 'od dpag med//yan lag drug las byung ba yi//dngos grub 'di ni khyad par 'phags//de nas bzlas pa 'am bsgom pa ni//dbang po rab 'bring tha ma ste//rab kyis yongs su rdzogs pa yi//bsgom pa 
6a1(rgyud): @#//
6b1(rgyud): pa las ma yin te//nam mkha'i me tog las ni dri//rgyu de rkyen med pa las min//nags sreg me la rlung med bzhin//de ltar yan lag bzhi ldan pa'i//las dang po yis rnam rtog spang //lha yi rnal 'byor la gnas 
6b2(rgyud): pas//bskyed pa zhes ni gsungs pa yin//phyi dang nang du du ma'i cha//de yi rim par bshad pa yin//rten gyi dkyil 'khor bye brag kyang //khyams dang bla gab med pa dang //bla gab bcas dang rnam pa gsum//mos pa'i dbang 
6b3(rgyud): du gsungs pa yin//'khor lo bzhi yi dbye ba yis//nyin mtshan bzhi ru gsungs pa yin//mtshams kyang bzhi ru shes par bya//dbyu gu chu tshod 'pho ba dang //thun brgyad bsgom pa'i rnal 'byor pas//thun dang mtshams 
6b4(rgyud): ni mnyam par gsungs//mtshams kyi tshe na rnal 'byor pa//dbang po 'bring dang tha ma yis//lha rnams rdzogs par spro byas la//mar me gcig las gnyis kyi tshul//mchod pa la sogs tshul bzhin bya//lta ba zhes pa 
6b5(rgyud): mnyam gzhag ste//rjes thob spyod pa ru ni bshad//bskyed pa'i rim pa bsgom pa las//rgyan drug 'chang ba'i spyod pa gnang //gsang ba'i dbang dang 'brel ba la//rgyan dang bdud rtsi'i spyod pa gnang //rdo rje'i rigs dang rin 
6b6(rgyud): chen rigs//pad ma'i rigs dang las kyi rigs//spyod pa gnad pa nga yis gsungs//shes rab ye shes dang 'brel bas//gong du gsungs pa thun mong ste//khyad par phyag rgya'i spyod pa gnad//lta ba mi ldan spyod pa dang //
6b7(rgyud): spyod pa mi ldan lta ba gnyis//'di ni gsang sngags dam sri ste//mnar med sems can dmyal bar skye//de phyir lta med spyod pa spang //srin po khros pa'i spyod pa'o//spyod med lta ba'ang spang pa ste//dka' thub 
6b8(rgyud): kyis ni lus gdungs shing //phung sogs rnam snang la sogs la//brnyas pa rtog ge skam po yin//des na lta spyod zung du 'jug/glang chen bzhengs kyang mnyam par bzhengs//glang chen bzhugs kyang mnyam par 
7a1(rgyud): @#//bzhugs//rdo rje snying pos yang zhus pa//bdud rtsi zhes pa ji ltar lags//de yi spyod pa ji ltar lags//de yin yon tan ji ltar 'byung //rgyan la sogs pa'i don rnams kun//gtso bos bdag la bshad du 
7a2(rgyud): gsol//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//bdud rtsi rnam pa gsum du gsungs//dang por phyi yi bdud rtsi bsten//bdud rtsi lnga dang sha lnga la//lcags kyu lnga ni dgod pa ste//brgyad dang bcu bzhi dang por 'dzag/si h+la mi bskyod 
7a3(rgyud): lud skam blang //bcu gnyis bcu drug dang por 'dzag/he ru ka dang rnam snang dang //lud skam snying por blang bar bya//yar gyi ngo dang mar gyi ngo//brgyad dang bcu bzhi bcwa lnga la//thams cad bzang po'i dus su gsungs//seng 
7a4(rgyud): ge'i gzugs ni dkar po bzang //lus ngag sdom zhing nyes pa med//'di ni sha yi bsgrub pa'i rten//glang po glang po chen po ste//ke ta ke yi rgyu nyid las//gla rtsi la sogs 'byung ba'i sha//ba lang gi wang ldan pa'i sha//rta ni rta yi 
7a5(rgyud): rgyal po ste//kha dog dkar min dmar ba min//dbyibs dang kha dog mi 'gyur sha//mi ni skye ba bdun pa'i sha//lus ngag yid la mkhas pas brtag/bza' ba'i snod ni dum bu gcig/de yi nang du bsgrub par bya//rab 'bring tha 
7a6(rgyud): ma'i mtshan ma ni//'bar dang dud pa lco ba rnams//mtshan ma phyi yi yin par bshad//gzhan skyes don ni rgas chen sel//skad ma phyin na dngos grub med//skad phyung bas ni tshangs par 'gyur//nang gi bdud rtsi rang byung yin//
7a7(rgyud): dril can bu ga dgu 'am nas//'dzag pa'i bdud rtsi rgyun chad nas//til gyi 'bru bzhin gang ba ste//a ba d+hU tIr dbugs chud pas//steng gi drug pa'i rgyu ba yin//si h+la ga bur dbus na gnas//'og gi drug pa rgyu ba yin//'di ni 
7a8(rgyud): gsang ba'i bdud rtsi te//skra dkar gnyer ma 'chi ba spong //spyod pa'i rnal 'byor rnam pa gnyis//gang zag mchog dang las dang po//skyed pa'i rim la legs gnas shing //rigs su 'dzin pa spang ba'i phyir//phyi yi 
7b1(rgyud): drug pa'i bdud rtsi sbyin//las dang po la phyi nang gnyis//'di yi nus pa'ang du ma ste//skra dkar med cing rnam rtog 'joms//dkyil 'khor bzhi la rab tu gnas//lnga pa goms par gyur pa yis//drug pa gzung bar 
7b2(rgyud): gsungs pa ste//kun rdzob steng gi sna rtse nas//sor bzhi nas ni dbus su bzhug/lte ba'i gnas su dbugs gnas pa//steng gi drug pa zin pa yin//'di yi nus pa'ang du ba ste//sred pa med cing yang ba'o//'dab chags rgyal 
7b3(rgyud): po ji bzhin du//nam mkhar 'phur zhing ltung ba med//mchog dang chad pa'i dbus gnas pa//'og gi drug pa yin par bshad//gang zag mchog la gsungs pa yin//'di yi nus ba'ang du ma ste//dril can bu ga 'gag 
7b4(rgyud): par byed//rgyan gyi dang po zhe sdang 'joms//dur khrod chen po sih+la ling //ru dra 'dul ba'i rim pa ste//bdud rtsis rnam rtog la sogs 'joms//phrin las bzhi ni thun mong yin//phyag rgyas 'dod chags rnam par dag/
7b5(rgyud): rdo rje snying pos yang gsol pa//drug pa zhes pa ji lta bu//gang zag mchog ni ji lta bu//'dod chags ji ltar rnam par dag/dpa' bos bdag la bshad du gsol//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//g.yas 
7b6(rgyud): g.yon steng 'og bzhi las ni//lnga pa nam mkha' 'byung ba yin//lnga pa rang bzhin med pa ste//drug pa zhes ni gsungs pa yin//dga' ba mchog dga' lhan cig dga'//bral ba'i dga' ba sngon 'gro bas//lnga pa bde 
7b7(rgyud): ba chen po 'byung //bde chen rang bzhin med pa dag/'og gi sgo yi drug pa yin//de phyir drug pa 'dod pas kyang //bzhi pa sngon du song ba yis//lnga pa yongs su rdzogs pa sgom//dper na zangs kyi dngos po la//gser 
7b8(rgyud): 'gyur rtsi yis nus pa yis//zangs nyid gser du gyur pa ste//zangs las ma gtogs gser nyid dang //lnga pa las gzhan drug pa ni//sangs rgyas kyis kyang ma gsungs so//lo dang zla ba zla ba phyed//nyin 
8a1(rgyud): @#//zhag dus drug dus gnyis la//skad cig re tsam drug pa 'byung //las dang po yi spyod yul min//dper na chu yi snod nang du//bdud rtsi thigs pa gcig gzhag pa//chu ru nyams su myong ba las//gcig shos 
8a2(rgyud): nyams su myong ba med//chu la rten nas bdud rtsi yang //nyams su myong bar byed pa ste//de bzhin spros byas lnga pa la//rten nas spros med drug pa 'byung //rgyud gzhan du ni nga yis gsungs//mtshan ma lnga 'am ro brgyad dam 
8a3(rgyud): sa brgyad non nas mngon shes ldan//las kyi phyag rgya yongs su spangs//ye shes phyag rgya la gnas shing //phyag rgya chen po la 'jug pa//'di ni gang zag mchog yin te//'di yis drug pa zin par bshad//'dod chags 
8a4(rgyud): zhes pa 'khor ba ste//rnam dag mya ngan 'das par gsungs//de nas gsang ba gnyis pa yang //gsang ba'i dbang dang 'brel ba ste//rten cing 'brel pa zab mo'i don//dang po lha yi nga rgyal bya//mkha' 'gro rtsa ru shes par 
8a5(rgyud): bya//dpa' bo byang chub sems su shes//'khor lo'i gnas dang 'khor lo dang //de nas bsgrub bya shes par bya//bde ba chen po'i 'khor lo nas//tshangs mdo'i bar du sor mo'i tshad//bcu tshan bzhi dang brgyad yin te//
8a6(rgyud): sor gsum la ni 'khor lo re//sku gsung thugs kyi rang bzhin yin//dbang gis gnas kyi 'khor lo'i gnas//bla ma'i zhal nas shes par bya//tshangs mdo me la gzhang kha rlung //sor gsum sor bzhi mkhas pas 
8a7(rgyud): brtag/gnas gsum gsang ba'i gnas yin te//de bzhin gnas gsum rig pa'o//sor bzhi rta mgrin gnas yin te//sor brgyad gsang ba bde skyong gnas//rnal 'byor pha dang rnal 'byor ma'i//lus kyi rten gyi 
8a8(rgyud): bye brag las//sor mo bzhi bcu brgyad nyid las//brgyad ni gsang ba'i 'khor lo'i gnas//bzhi bcu la ni 'khor lo gzhan//bla ma'i zhal nas shes byar bya//thams cad sgab tshigs dbyug gu ste//phyi dang nang du mkhas 
8b1(rgyud): pas brtag/'khor lo la yang rnam pa gnyis//dang por 'khor lo gnyis pa rtsa//rkyen dang gsang ba rang bzhin dang //dbang dang rnam pa bzhi ru gsungs//rkyen la gnyis te gsang ba bzhi//rang bzhin bcu drug dbang ni 
8b2(rgyud): gnyis//tsan Da li ni nyi shu bzhi//'khor lo bzhi ru sbas pa ste//bla ma tshogs nang mthong na rgyab kyi phyogs//man ngag gzhan du spel zhing 'phyar la dga'//'dod chags spyod phyir sbas pa'i rim pa nod//gab pa'i 
8b3(rgyud): don la rgyab kyi phyogs shing skur pa 'debs//gsang sngags bar du gcod cing rnal 'byor gzhan la gshe//'di ni mnar med sems can dmyal ba chen por skye//sangs rgyas stong dang rtsa gcig rdzogs pa tshe yi tshad//
8b4(rgyud): 'di la sbas kyi thams cad la//nga yis sba bar ma gsungs te//de phyir rgyud gzhan du ni sbas//rgyud 'dir nga yis rgyas par bshad//sum bcu rtsa gnyis g.yas g.yon cha//drug bcu rtsa bzhi grangs su sbas//sum bcu 
8b5(rgyud): rtsa gnyis las ni zung //bcu drug grangs su sbas pa yin//sum bcu rtsa gnyis phyogs dang mtshams//brgyad kyi grangs su sbas pa'o//spyi gtsug 'khor lo spyi bor sbas//gsang ba bde skyong lte bar sbas//bcu drug las 
8b6(rgyud): ni zung 'gyur ba//brgyad kyi grangs su sbasapa yin//bcu drug las ni g.yas g.yon cha//sum bcu rtsa gnyis grangs su sbas//bcu drug las ni bzhi bzhir 'gyur//drug bcu rtsa bzhi'i grangs su sbas//mgrin pa 
8b7(rgyud): longs spyod 'khor lo ru//ro drug la ni longs spyod pas//ro ma longs spyod 'khor lor sbas//rta mgrin 'khor lo mgrin par sbas//brgyad las g.yas g.yon cha gnyis cha//bcu drug grangs su spas pa yin//brgyad 
8b8(rgyud): las g.yas g.yon steng 'og bzhi//sum bcu rtsa gnyis grang su sbas//brgyad las g.yas bzhi steng 'og bzhi//mtshams bzhi dang ni brgyad kyi cha//drug bcu rtsa bzhi grangs su sbas//tshangs mdo'i me ni 
9a1(rgyud): @#//me la spas//snying ga chos kyi 'khor lo ru//chos kun rig pas rig pa spas//drug bcu rtsa bzhi las ni zung //sum bcu rtsa gnyis grangs su spas//drug bcu rtsa bzhilas ni bzhi//bcu drug grangs su spas pa 
9a2(rgyud): yin//drug bcu rtsa bzhi las mi brgyad//brgyad kyi grangs su spas pa'o//gzhang kha rlung ni rlung las spas//gsang ba'i gtum mo gtum mor spas//'khor lo re la 'khor lo bzhi//phyi ru srog gi rnam par 'khyud//
9a3(rgyud): chu dang chu bran chu bo rnams//sna tshogs du mar 'bab byed kyang //rgya mtsho chen por ro cig 'gyur//lte bar rol pa bde chen sku//rnam par thar dang stong pa dang //de bzhin mtshan dang dpe byad ldan//snying ga chos kyi sku yin 
9a4(rgyud): te//rnam thar la sogs de bzhin no//mgrin pa longs spyod de dang 'dra//bde chen sprul pa'ang de bzhin no//mtshan rnams steng la stong pa 'og/dang po'i bde chen kha sbyor yin//rnam thar steng la dpe byad 'og/bde chen 
9a5(rgyud): kha sbyor gnyis pa yin//gzhan ni zhal las shes par bya//stobs bcu rnam dag kha sbyor bcu//rlung ni sgrol ma'i ngo bo ste//rtsa drug dbugs kyi rang bzhin gang //me ni gos dkar rang bzhin te//zur rtsa gsum ni 
9a6(rgyud): drod kyis gang //rang bzhin gyis ni gnas pa la//brgyad dang lnga yi mtshan nyid la//brgyad la dbyangs kyi dang po brgyad//lnga la kA li dang po lnga//rig pa'i pho brang mtha' can lnga//rta mgrin dbyangs yig g.yon dang 
9a7(rgyud): g.yas//a gnyis mthar bzhag 'dab dgu yin//gsang ba'i bde skyong 'khor lo ni//ma ning bzhi dang a gnyis dbus//byang chub sum bcu rtsa bdun yin//de nas rtsa yi mtshan nyid kyang //phyi yis stong phrag bdun bcu gnyis 
9a8(rgyud): sha la gnas nas tshil bu bskyed//nang gi brgya dang nyi shu ni//tshil la gnas nas rkang bskyed pa'o//gsang ba sum bcu rtsa gnyis ni//rus par gnas shing ga bur bskyed//de kho na yi rtsa gsum ni//gsang bar gnas 
9b1(rgyud): shing bde ba skyad//khang pa stong phrag bdun bcu gnyis//khang pa re la khang pa 'bum//khang pa der gnas srin bu'i rigs//stong phrag bdun bcu rtsa gnyis te//grangs ni 'bum du shes par bya//nang dang gsang ba'i 
9b2(rgyud): rtsa la yang //srin bu'i rigs ni grangs su gsungs//de kho na'i rtsa gsum las//'dod chags bskyed pa'i srin bu dgu//nyin dang mtshan mo mtshams dang gsum//dgu ru mkhas pas shes par bya//rtsa der 
9b3(rgyud): gnas pa'i ga bur yang //bdud rtsi sbyor ba las byung ba//bdud rtsi la sogs gong du gsungs//yaM las rlung gi dkyil 'khor dang //raM las me yi dkyil 'khor dang //de steng a las ka pA la//paM las pad ma 
9b4(rgyud): 'dab brgyad bsam//nang du zla ba'i dkyil 'khor bsam//de ru sha la sogs pa dgod//gtso bo'i steng du sor bzhi'am//mtho gang tsam du bsam pa ni//yi ge go bzlog bsam par bya//sngon po dmar po dkar po ste//hUM 
9b5(rgyud): las gyur pa'i rdo rje bsam//de steng sa yi ge oM bsams la//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor steng du bsam//'di ni bdud rtsi'i kha sbyor ro//sha lnga las ni gdan lngar gyur//lcags kyu bdud rtsi lnga ru 'gyur//las kyi rlung gis 
9b6(rgyud): me bus te//zla ba gtso bo la sogs zhu//tha mal pa yi bdud rtsi yin//yi ge gsum dang nyi ma zhu//lha yi bdud rtsi zhes ni bya//'od zer gyis bkug ye shes kyi//bdud rtsi yin par nga yis gsungs//lan gsum skor 
9b7(rgyud): ba sku gsung thugs//'di ni byin rlabs rnal 'byor ro//de nas bdud rtsi myang dang mchod//mchod pa'i rnal 'byor zhes ni gsungs//de nas rol pa'i rnal 'byor te//g.yas g.yon dgongs pa shes par bya//de nas zhu ba'i 
9b8(rgyud): rnal 'byor yang //mnyam pa'i rlung dang gtum mo'i mes//ye shes bzhi yi ngo bor gyur//cha cig mi skyod rnam snang ste//sha za'i mkha' 'gro mnyam pa nyid//cha cig bya ba nan tan te//srin bu las kyi mkha' 'gro mchod//
10a1(rgyud): @#//
10b1(rgyud): thim nas//nyi ma zhabs kyu la thim bsam//zhabs kyu la sogs rim par thim//yan lag lnga ni ye shes lnga//de ni phra mo'i rnal 'byor te//de nas nA da phra mo la//sems ni rim par bsgom pa'o//de nas rang 
10b2(rgyud): rig ye shes te//chos kun stong par ro gcig pa'o//gzhan yang chos kyi 'khor lo dang //sprul pa yi ni 'khor lo gnyis//'bar ba dang ni 'dzag pa'i tshul//bla ma'i zhal nas shes par bya//phyi yi rnam rtog rtsol ba 
10b3(rgyud): ste//nang gi rnam rtog srog gi rlung //srog rtsol gnyis 'gags dbu ma ste//kun rdzob steng gi sna rtse nas//sor bzhi nas ni rtsa gsum gcig/dbugs dgag pa yi gnas yin pas//dang por de ru gzung par bya//
10b4(rgyud): de nas sprul pa'i gnas su bzung //yang na grang par shes par bya//de nas gsang ba gsum pa ni//shes rab ye shes dang 'brel ba'i//dga' ba'i mtshan nyid bshad pa'o//gzhang kha g.ya' zhing ba spu ldang //dga' 
10b5(rgyud): ba'i mtshan nyid mi dmigs pa//bcu gnyis rang bzhin yin par gsungs//byang chub sems rgyu mi rtog snyam//mchog dga' mi dmigs nyi shu lnga//gsang ba'i nor bu yongs su gang //lhan cig skyes dga' 
10b6(rgyud): mi dmigs pa//chos kyi sku ni yin par gsungs//bde ba'i rgyun chad bral ba'i dga'//de yi mi dmigs gzugs sku gnyis//gzhan yang sngon du 'gro ba rnams//yan lag thabs dang sbyor ba dngos//dgongs 
10b7(rgyud): pa dang bcas rim pa'o//skar ma'i tshogs ltar du mar spros mod kyang //nyi ma zla ba lta bur zung du 'jug/nyi ma zla ba lta bur zungs 'jug kyang //sgra can lta bur gnyis su med pa'o//rdo rje snying pos gsol pa//skar ma'i 
10b8(rgyud): tshogs ni ji lta bu//zung du 'jug pa ji lta bu//gnyis su med pa ji lta bu//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//byang chub sum bcu rtsa bdun ni//rgyu'i khyad par spros pa'o//sku gsum dang ni ye shes lnga//'bras 
11a1(rgyud): @#//bu'i khyad par spros pa yin//rgyu rnams bdun du bsdu ba dang //'bras bu gsum du bsdu ba'o//rgyu rnams 'bras bu la thim ste//'bras bu gnyis su bsgom par bya//chos kun sems kyi ngo bo ste//sems ni 
11a2(rgyud): stong pa'i rang bzhin yin//thams cad stong par ro gcig pa//'di ni blang dor med pa'i dbyings//'dod chags rnam par dag bya'i phyir//'dod chags sku yi phyag rgya lnga//phyag rgya chen po ye shes gnyis//dam tshig chos 
11a3(rgyud): dang las phyag rgya//'di la rten pa shin du zab//'di ni bde ba chen po ste//bskyed dang rdzogs pa'i rang bzhin dag/bde chen 'khor lo rnam pa gsum//gsang ba'i sna rtse e la gnas//sprul pa'i 'khor lo gang ba yin//
11a4(rgyud): rig pa la sogs de bzhin te//bsang ba bde skyong ba la gnas//rang bzhin bde chen 'khor lo ste//bde ba chen pos rol gdab//til dang til mar ji bzhin no//dper na shing sna bcad pa las//'o ma 'dzag pa ji bzhin no//
11a5(rgyud): bde ba chen po de nyid las//skra yi rtse mo brgyad stong dang //khri dang 'bum gyis sgom pa ste//dang po sprin gyi rnam pa ste//gnyis pa du ba lta bu'o//gsum pa glog gi rnam pa ste//bzhi pa mar me lta bu'o//'di dag kun gyi rim 
11a6(rgyud): pa yang //sprul pa la sogs rim pa bzhin//sprin gyi rnam pa skye ba ni//a ni skye ba'i mtshan ma yin//mar me lta bu 'chi ba yis//ha~M gi mtshan mar gsungs pa yin//lnga pa nam mkha' lta bu ni//gnyis su med par bar 
11a7(rgyud): mdo'i//mtshan ma stong par shes par bya//gzhan yang 'dar ba la sogs pa//bla ma'i zhal nas shes par bya//de nas gsang ba gsum pa yang //shes rab ye shes 'brel ba ste//dga' ba bzhi yi mtshan nyid kyang //
11a8(rgyud): gzhad lam g.ya' zhing ba spu ldang //lus 'dar dga' ba'i ngo bo yin//gsang ba rdo rje rtsal ma nas//byang chub sems rgyu bde ba snyam//mchog dga' yi ni ngo bo ste//rdo rje pad ma 'gas pa snyam//lhan cig skyes pa'i 
11b1(rgyud): ngo bo yin//bde ba'i rgyun chad bde ba snyam//bral ba dga' ba'i ngo bo yin//'di rnams kun gyi mtshan nyid kyang //byang chub sems phyogs la sogs pa//gong du gsungs par shes par bya//dga' ba snang ba zhes bya ste//
11b2(rgyud): mchog dga' snang ba mched pa yin//rab tu dga' ba snang ba thob//lhan cig skyes pa 'od gsal ba'o//ye shes bzhi yi rang bzhin te//bdud bzhi 'joms pa'i nges pa yin//rigs pa bzhi ni gsang bar 'joms//
11b3(rgyud): phra ba'i bdud bzhi rang bzhin gyis//'khor lo bzhi ru 'joms pa yin//'khor lo la rten dga' ba bzhi//de nyid ye shes bzhi ru snang //de phyir bdud bzhi 'joms pa yin//'dod chags la rten du gsum ni//nyon 
11b4(rgyud): mongs pa yi bdud yin te//'dod chags rnam pa bcu gnyis yin//gsang ba'i bde skyong nyon mongs 'joms//kha dog dbyibs ni phung po'i bdud//rta mgrin 'khor lo phung po 'joms//sbas pa bzhi ni lha yi bdud//rig 
11b5(rgyud): pa'i 'khor lo lha bdud 'joms//skye ba dang bcas 'chi ba ste//gsang ba skye med 'chi bdag 'joms//phra ba'i bdud 'joms de dang 'dra//skad cig bzhi dang sde pa bzhi//bzang po bzhi dang bden pa bzhi//'byung ba 
11b6(rgyud): bzhi dang yum bzhi dang //tshangs pa'i gnas bzhi la sogs pa//ye shes bzhi po 'di nyid las//khyad par gzhan ni mi 'byung ste//rnam par rtog pa'i bye brag dang //gzhan yang mos pa'i bye brag dang //chos tshul 
11b7(rgyud): du ma'i bye brag gis//sna tshogs du mar gsungs pa yin//dbang gsum bgrod pa'i rnal 'byor pa//dge slong chen por gsungs pa ste//'di yi thams cad bya bar gsungs//rdo rje snying pos yang gsol pa//dge 
11b8(rgyud): slong dbang gsum ji ltar lags//de yi bya ba ji lta bu//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//dge slong la sogs rnam pa gsum//so sor thar dang byang chub sems//rig 'dzin nyid kyi dge slong ngo //gcig dang

12b1(rgyud): nyid bsam du med pas na/cir yang bsam par mi bya'o//sems nyid stong par bcu gnas su//stong pa sems su rab bcu gnas//stong dang mi stong la sogs pa'i//rtog pa bcom pas mkha' ltar 'gyur//rten yod 
12b2(rgyud): ma yin rten pa med//dpe med gnas kyang yod ma yin//de nyid gnas kyang rdzogs pa ste//lhan skyes gnyis med rig pa'o//de nas gcod pa bka' stsal pa//khro tshul gar dang phyag rgya rnams//sgyu ma tsam du ma shes

12b7(rgyud): sgong lta bu dang //dbyings dang ye shes la sogs pa//lta ba'i don nyid bcad pa'o//rgyud kyi don ni rnam pa gsum//bde chen 'khor lo rgyu yi rgyud//gsang ba'i sna rtse thabs kyi rgyud//gsang ba bde skyong 'bras bu'i rgyud//bum pa

13b2(rgyud): yin//bde gshegs kun gyi thugs kyi gsang //de ni nga yis bshad kyis nyon//rtag chad skye 'gag la sogs pa'i//mtha' spangs sems nyid rang rig cing //gdod nas spros bral bden pa nyid//de don bsam gyis mi khyab 
13b3(rgyud): nges//de nyid dbye ba rab 'bring mtha'//rnam pa gsum du shes bya ste//dang por rang rig bsam mi khyab//rgyu dang lam rtags bsam mi khyab//mtha' dang nyon mongs bsam mi khyab//chos dang lta spyod bsam mi khyab//yi ge 
13b4(rgyud): bsam gyis mi khyab pa//mdor bstan de nyid rnam par dbye//gzugs sgra dri ro reg bya chos//de nyid chos ni bsam mi khyab//a ni yi ge dam pa ste//khong nas 'byung ba skye ba med//skye ba kun gyi rgyur gyur pa//yi ge dam pa 
13b5(rgyud): bsam mi khyab//phrag byung khrus dang gtsang sbra dang //rgyan cha gcer bu thal gos sogs//chang dang bdud rtsi sha dang yang //ma dang sring mo bu mo sogs//rol pa'i spyod pa bsam mi khyab//nga yis 'dul ba'i sems can la//mos pa'i 
13b6(rgyud): mtha' la thug pa med//de phyir theg pa thug med pas//de nyid lta ba bsam mi khyab//rtag chad sgro skur ltos pa'i sgra//de nyid mtha' gnas bsam mi khyab//'dod chags zhe sdang gti mug sogs//nyon mongs bsam gyis mi khyab pa//
13b7(rgyud): 'dar dang 'phar dang drod la sogs//rtags nyid bsam gyis mi khyab pa/lha bsgom rlung dang rtsa sogs pa//lam nyid bsam gyis mi khyab pa //rang rig nam mkha'i sprin char tshul//pha rol phyin drug la sogs pa//rgyu nyid bsam 
13b8(rgyud): gyis mi khyab pa//rgyu 'bras tha dad kun dang bral//rtsol ba rdul gyis ma gos pa//sprin med mkha' ltar ma bcos pa'i//spyi dang rang gi mtshan nyid dag/rang rig bsam gyis mi khyab pa'o//a ni shing dang sbrul sogs kyi//
14a1(rgyud): bcu gnyis la sogs dbye ba yis//yi ge'i dbye ba rgyas pa'o//gzugs ni sdug dang mi sdug sogs//bcu dgu phra rab rdul gyi bar//sgra la sogs pa de bzhin te//sgra brgyad dri gsum ro drug ste//reg bya 
14a2(rgyud): bcu gcig chos de bzhin//gang zhig 'dus byas 'dus ma byas//chos kyi dbye ba rgyas pa'o//theg pa gsum dang bco brgyad dang //ji snyed 'du shes yod kyi bar//lta ba'i dbye bas rgyas pa'o//tshogs kyi lam dang sbyor ba'i lam//
14a3(rgyud): mthong lam bsgom lam mthar phyin lam//lam gyi dbye ba rgyas pa'o//sems rtags bdud rtags 'byung ba'i rtags//rtags kyi dbye ba rgyas pa'o/mu stegs rang rgyal nyan thos nas//rdo rje 'dzin pa'i spyod pa'i bar//spyod 
14a4(rgyud): pa'i dbye ba rgyas pa'o//rtag dang chad dang sgro skur nas//'khor dang mya ngan 'das mtha' dang //thabs dang shes rab mtha yi bar//mtha' yi dbye bas rgyas pa'o//'dod chags la sogs nyi khri dang //tshig stong rtsa ba nye ba dang //
14a5(rgyud): zhe sdang la sogs de bzhin te//nyon mongs dbye ba rgyas pa'o//kun nas nyon mongs rnam par byang //'dod chags che dang sbyin pa sogs//rgyu yi dbye ba rgyas pa'o//semasad dang nam mkha' ye shes dang //sangs rgyas 'od gsal 
14a6(rgyud): zung 'jug sogs//rang rig dbye ba rgyas pa'o//blos brtags mtshan mar snang ba dang //sgro 'dogs pa dang skur 'debs dang //rim min mos pa khyad par tshol//rdul tsam khas len blos 'jog cing //sangs rgyas nyid kyang 
14a7(rgyud): sgrub byed pa//gang ltar bcos kyang de mtha' ste//gang zhig mthar lhung lta ba de//rang rig gzhan rig sems ma yin//kun rdzob don dam gnyis ma yin//rang rig rtog gi skam po dang //mthar lhung dbu mar khas len pa//de 
14a8(rgyud): ni sems nyid rang rig dang //brjod med bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i//mi mthun pa yi phyogs yin te//de bral bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i/rang rig ye shes chen po yin//sbyin sogs dkar po'i chos rnams dang //rang rig 
14b1(rgyud): spyi yi rnam pa yis//rang rig ye shes chen po la//thabs dang thabs las byung ba 'am//rgyu dang 'bras bu la sogs pa//rdul tsam tha snyad yod ma yin//rang 'byung ye shes yin pa'i phyir//'dod chags la sogs skyon gyis kyang 
14b2(rgyud): de la sgrib pa ma yin te//nam mkha' la ni sprin bzhin no//rang rig gzhan rig bye brag gis//sems can sangs rgyas gnyis su dbye//rang rig sprin med nam mkha' la//gzhan rig rnam rtog nam mkhar mtshungs//sprin tshogs 
14b3(rgyud): rlung gis gtor byas te//sprin med mkha' 'dra snang ba nyid//gzhan rig rnam rtog bral ba la//sgrib bral sangs rgyas zhes bya ste//rang rig kho na tsam du zad//dper na skyes bu nam mkha' lta ba la//sprin gyis skyes bu'i mig 
14b4(rgyud): la bsgribs pa las//nam mkha' de la ma sgribs ji bzhin no//grangs ni dang po gcig yin te//gzhan rig rang rig gyur pa las//dag pa gzhan gyis rnam grol min//'dir ni rang rig gcig kho na//de nyid rig cing nges pa'o//
14b5(rgyud): kun kyang nyon mongs rnam par byang //gzung 'dzin thabs dang shes rab ste//bsam gyis mi khyab gnyis pa'o//lus ngag sems kyi rang bzhin las//rnam thar stong dang mtshan ma med//smon pa med dang sku gsung thugs//bsam 
14b6(rgyud): gyis mi khyab gsum pa'o//byams dang snying rje dga' btang snyoms//sa chu me rlung rang bzhin las//bzhi yi grangs su nges pa ste//dga' ba la sogs de bzhin no//rigs lnga yi ni sangs rgyas lnga//phung po lnga dang chags sdang 
14b7(rgyud): lnga//bsam gyis mi khyab lnga pa'o//pha rol phyin drug rang bzhin las//yul drug dbang po drug la sogs//bsam gyis mi khyab drug nyid de//brgyad sogs grangs ni bsam mi khyab//gang zhig rang rig gzhan rig gnyis//thog 
14b8(rgyud): ma med nas gnas pa ste//lus dang grib ma'i tshul du gnas//rang rig med par gzhan rig dag/so so skye bo'i dus na 'dod//'di yang rmongs chen ma rungs pa//gzhan rig ma spangs rang rig 'dod//'khor ba ma spangs mya ngan

15b1(rgyud): mnyam gzhag rjes thob kun spangs te//sgyu ma'i tshul du gang gnas pa//snang ba mthar phyin pa yi tshad//thun dang 'pho ba chu tshod dang //dbyu gu skad cig rim pa la//sems kyi bying rgod la sogs skyon//bral te dben par gnas pa

15b3(rgyud): nyid la//mthar phyin pa yi tshad yin no//'dod chags ro yis myos pa dang //sems bskyed sdom 'dzin dbang bskur dang //tshogs kyi 'khor lo 'dus pa'i dus//bsam gyis mi khyab rgyal po'i dus//khyad par can du gsungs 
15b4(rgyud): pa'o//gzhan yang rtog pa'i sems dang yang //rtog med sems dang 'dres pa yi//gzugs la rten pa'i mig nyid kyis//rtog pa'i rgyun chad sems de nyid//sems kyi rgyun bral rgyu de nyid//bsam gyis mi khyab rgyu yi dus//sgra la 
15b5(rgyud): sogs pa'i de bzhin no//'dod chags zhe sdang gti mug sogs//nyon mongs rjes nas rgyu ba ste//byams dang sbyin sogs de bzhin no//gzo sogs btang snyoms de bzhin te//bsam gyis mi khyab sgyu yi dus//bla ma'i zhal nas 
15b6(rgyud): shes par bya//sbyin dang tshul khrims la sogs dang //byams dang snying rje la sogs pa'i//mdor na dkar po'i chos rnams dang //gzo dang rgyu rtsal la sogs dang //bskyed rdzogs bsgom pa'i rim pa kun//skye ba gcig gam du mas

15b8(rgyud): rnams bsam gyis mi khyab pas//bar ma chod par 'jug pa'i dus//gzung ba'i thabs ni bco brgyad de//mkha' mnyam gzhi bcas gzhi med dang //yul drug dug gsum rten pa dang //lhan cig skyes nyid sgra la brten//sa chu 
16a1(rgyud): @#//me rlung sgra brten no//chos rnams gzod nas ma skyes pa'i//rang rig bsgom pa mkha' mnyam ste//bla ma'i sems bzung dang por gces//chos rnams thams cad sems yin ste//sems ni gzod nas ma skyes pa//
16a2(rgyud): gzhi bcas rang rig rnal 'byor ro//sems ni gzod nas rang rig pa//gzhi med bsam gyis mi khyab pa'o//gzugs sgra dri ro reg bya chos//de brten sems bzung bla ma las//skyes bus 'bad de blang bar bya//de bzhin 'dod chags 
16a3(rgyud): zhe sdang dang //gti mug rjes su mkhas pas bzung //lhan cig skyes gnyis 'byung bzhi la//brten te skye ba med par bsgom//bye brag sems bzung bsam mi khyab//nyan thos la sogs theg chen la//man ngag yod pa ma yin te//
16a4(rgyud): lta ba spyod pa sems gzung dang //rgyu dang 'bras bu lam dang yang //dus dang longs spyod rnal 'byor dang //bdag don gzhan don dgongs pa dang //ngal dub mtha' spangs tshogs chung dang //tshegs sogs pa yi khyad par dang //khyad par 
16a5(rgyud): rnam pa bcwa lnga yis//rig 'dzin theg pa khyad par 'phags//kye ho bcom ldan 'jigs pa che//rang rig bsam mi khyab pa la//sbyin dang byams sogs dgongs pa ci//sku gnyis gzhan don gang las byung //bsam mi khyab la 
16a6(rgyud): legs gnas pas//de nyid ci lags phyi ru ci//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//sbyin dang byams pa la sogs pas//gzhan rig rnam rtog 'gags pa dang //sku gnyis sems can don mdzad pa'i//sems can rnams 
16a7(rgyud): ni sangs rgyas su//'byung ba'i rkyen du nges pas na//bsam mi khyab la mi gnas kyang //sbyin sogs 'bad de bslab par bya//de la bsam mi khyab pa 'byung //sku gnyis stobs dang mi 'jigs dang //gzungs dang ting 'dzin las rnams 
16a8(rgyud): kun//sbyin la sogs pa sbyin byas nas//bsam mi khyab pa 'byung bar nges//de nyid rtogs na bsam mi khyab//de nyid ma rtogs 'khor ba'i chos//dbul pos nor bu'i byi dor bzhin//bsam mi khyab la sems gzhag kyang //
16b1(rgyud): bskyed rdzogs rim pas sems gnas nas//de ni sngon sbyangs 'phro can te//sems kyi yi ge mgo gcad bag ces//rang sems las su rung ba na//de ni phyi rtags nang du ste//de nyid nang rtags phyi ru 'byung //lus la dri med srin 
16b2(rgyud): bu med//skra dkar gnyer ma sogs med pa//bskyed dang rdzogs pa'i rtags yin no//'khor ba sgyu mar snang ba dang //sprin med mkha' ltar gang snang ba//bsam mi khyab pa'i rtags yin no//de nas 'khor 'dus pa de dag 
16b3(rgyud): thams cad chos thams cad kyi rang bzhin de kho na nyid bsam gyis mi khyab pa la rjes su yi rang bar gyur to//dpal 'khor lo sdom pa'i gsang ba bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po//bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs pa rdzogs 
16b4(rgyud): paN Di ta gA ya d+ha ra dang /lo ts+tsha ba shA kya ye shes kyis bsgyur pa'o////@#//rgya gar skad du/shrI khA sa ma tan tra rA dza nA ma/bod skad du/dpal nam mkha' dang mnyam pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po zhes bya ba dpal 
16b5(rgyud): bde mchog la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje btsun mo'i b+ha ga rnams la bzhugs so//de'i tshe 'khor 'dus 
16b6(rgyud): pa de dag gis nang nas mkha' spyod ma dang //sa spyod ma dang //sa 'og na spyod pa ma gsum la bcom ldan 'das kyis zhal 'dzum pa mdzad de//gsang ba'i don 'di gsungs pa//dad ldan snying rjer ldan dang rtse gcig sems//lus ngag 
16b7(rgyud): gus bcas gzhan la phan sems ldan//'jigs med rtul zhugs ldan zhing zab mo yi//lam la spro bar gyis la legs par nyon//mkha' 'gro khyod kyi mtshan nyid ni//nga yis khyod la bshad par bya//mkha' spyod steng sgo'i rlung 
16b8(rgyud): yin te//sa spyod bar du thig le'i sgo//sa 'og spyod pa shu kra'i sgo//sgo gsum sku gsung thugs yin te//dang po mkha' spyod mtshan nyid kyang //rnam pa gsum du shes bya ste//bems po'i rlung dang rnam rtog rlung //kun gyi 
17a1(rgyud): @#//mtshan nyid ye shes rlung //mdor bstan de nyid rim pas dbye//gnyis med rlung ni ma ning ste//zung 'jug thabs dang shes rab rlung //ldang 'jug sku gsung thugs kyi rlung //snying dang gzhang kha mgrin pa dang //lte ba lus 
17a2(rgyud): kyis khyab pa ru//rigs lnga'i rlung ni khyab par gnas//rgyu ba bzhi ni 'byung ba'i rlung //dangs snyigs 'byed pa brgyad du 'gyur//'di nyid rlung ni thun brgyad de//thun re la ni 'pho ba gnyis//rlung gi 'pho ba bcu drug yin//'pho ba gcig 
17a3(rgyud): la chu tshod gnyis//chu tshod re la dbyu gu gnyis//rlung gi dbyu gu drug cu bzhi//de bzhin chu tshod sum cu gnyis//nyi khri cig stong drug brgya sogs//sems dang sems min 'dres pa'i rlung //rags pa dang ni phra ba la//mkhas pas 
17a4(rgyud): sems kyi bzhon par gzung //rnam rtog gzung 'dzin sems kyi rta//gzung 'dzin gnyis 'gags dbu mar gnas//de nyid ye shes rdo rje'i rlung //dbye ba thabs dang shes rab gnyis//'di rnams mkha' spyod rang 
17a5(rgyud): bzhin te//de dag man ngag mkhas pas brtag/yon tan skyon dang rtags rnams kyis//rlung gi mtshan nyid shes par bya//de dag kun gyi rten nyid kyang //gzhol ma dang ni g.yon ma dang //'di ni gzhu yi rnam par gnas//bems 
17a6(rgyud): po rlung gi lam yin te//dbang po mngon sum lam yin pas//sna yi ri rab gling bzhi la//nyi zla byang chub lhor rgyug gis//thabs dang shes rab skor sogs la//'bad pas sems ni bzung bar bya//'di dag bems pos yang dag 
17a7(rgyud): gnas//mi shes pa yi rab rib kyis//rin chen sems ni dri can byas//'di ni rnam rtog rlung yin te//'di nyid kyis ni lam gyur pa//ro ma dang ni rkyang ma'o//sbrul 'khyil lta bu'i rnam pa ste//rnal 'byor gyi ni dmigs pa'i 
17a8(rgyud): yul//gnas der rnam rtog rlung 'gag go/rang rig gdod nas ma skyes pa//'di nyid ye shes rdo rje'i rlung //kun 'dar ma yi rtsal rten//tsan Da li ni shing lta bu//rang rig ye shes spyod yul te//gnas der 'bad de rang rig 
17b1(rgyud): gzung //mkha' spyod rlung gi rang bzhin phyir//'di dag rdo rje'i lam yin no//de nas sa spyod bshad bya ste//rang rig ye shes gang 'dod pas//tsaN Da li yi bye brag brtag/dang por rang bzhin tsaN Da li//gnyis pa gtum mo tsaN Da 
17b2(rgyud): li//gsum pa kha sbyar tsaN Da li//de bzhin gcer bu'i tsan Da li//de nyid la yang dbye ba ste//gcer bu gtum mo shin du gtum//yang dag gtum mo gzhan sbyar dang //bye brag kun la sbyar bar bya//gcer bu shin tu gcer bu 
17b3(rgyud): dang //shin tu gcer bu gcer bu sogs//'di dag kun la sbyar bar bya//dbye na rnam pa bcu gnyis te//yan lag bcu tshan bzhi dang brgyad//sa spyod ma yi rang bzhin te//stong phrag bdun cu rtsa gnyis sogs//gzhan skyes 
17b4(rgyud): kyi ni khro ma tsam phra ba de las bzlog pa'o//brgya dang nyi shu skra yi rtse//sum cu rtsa gnyis skra yi rtse//lnga yi cha ste gtso bo gsum//gsum 'dus yungs kar gyi ni tshad//dbus dang g.yas g.yon phra sbom dang //gnyis 'gyur bye brag shes 
17b5(rgyud): par bya//dangs ma dang ni snyigs ma'i rtsar//rang bzhin du ni gnas pa la//yi ge lta bu'i rtsa mdud kyang //dang po'i sgra ru gang shes pa//sa spyod ma yi rang bzhi no//gnas la sogs pa gzhan nas shes//'di nyid nor bu rin po che//

17b7(rgyud): cad kun gyi rten yin no//sa 'og spyod ma'i rang bzhin te//de nas yang dag rab bshad pa//byang chub sems ni rnam pa gsum//dang po chos nyid sems yin te//'di ni shes brjod yul las 'das//gnyis pa rang rig byang chub 
17b8(rgyud): sems//dper na rkyen gyis shel sgong bzhin//gsum pa bems po'i byang chub sems//si h+la dang ni ga pur ste//don dam kun rdzob gnyis su gnas//las kyi rlung dang las kyi mes//g.yo ba kun rdzob byang chub sems//
18a1(rgyud): @#//ye shes rlung dang ye shes mes//g.yo ba don dam sems yin te//dangs snyigs las ni rim pa yin//dbyibs dang kha dog las kyis ni//sems kyi khyad par shes par bya//las kyi rlung gis mi g.yo zhing //las

19b7(rgyud): bye brag ni//phyi yi tshe yi bcud kyis len//grub na 'chi bdag bdud 'joms so//de bzhin lus kyi phung po ste//longs spyod kyis ni nyon mongs 'joms//sems kyis lha yi bdud bcom ste//rten dang brten ni sgyu mar ldan//
19b8(rgyud): de nas gsang dbang bdud bzhi yang //phyag mtshan khro tshul gar la sogs//phyi ru du ma phung po'i bdud//rags pas yid ni nyon mongs pas//nyon mongs pa yis bdud yin no//dman pa'i lam gang ston pa yi//bla ma lha yi 
20a1(rgyud): bu yi bdud//phyi yi tshe yi bcud kyis len//de nyid 'chi bdag bdud yin no//gzhan yang bdud kyi bye brag ni//sgo gsum 'khrul par gang ston pa//bla ma lha yi bu yi bdud//sgo gsum las ni du ma'i 
20a2(rgyud): cha//de nyid ma shes phung po'i bdud//de nyid 'bar de bsgrub byas kyang //'bras bu mi 'byung sems nyon mongs//nyon mongs pa yi bdud yin te//rlung sogs gnas pa'i tshul shes pas//lha yi bu yi bdud bcom mo//
20a3(rgyud): de nyid sgyu mar shes par bya//de nyid shes pas phung po dang //nyon mongs pa yi bdu da bcom mo//dngos po'i mthar lhung skye bas 'chi//rgyu med las skye 'chi ba ste//rgyu rkyen ma bskyed rang bzhin gnas//sgyu mas 'chi bdag 
20a4(rgyud): bdud bcom mo//tshe la dbang sgyur ma lta bu//gnyen po'i stobs dang ldan pa'o//shes rab ye shes bdud bzhi yang //rtsa dang shu kra la sogs pa'i//dman pa'i lam ston lha yi bdud//de nyid du ma phung po'i bdud//de sgom 
20a5(rgyud): rang bzhin nyon mongs pas//nyon mongs pa yi bdus yin no//kha dog dbyibs ni skyes pas 'jig/de nyid 'chi bdag bdud yin no//gzhan yang bdud kyi bye brag kyang //'dod chags chen po'i lam ston pa'i//bla ma lha yi 
20a6(rgyud): bu'i bdud//dga' ba bzhi ni du ma ste//rgyud la spungs pas phung po'i bdud//rang sems nyon mongs nyon mongs bdud//'khor ba ltung bas skye ba ste//skye ba las byung 'chi bdag bdud//rnam par rtog pa skye bas so//
20a7(rgyud): 'dod chags kyis ni 'chi bdag bdud//ma spangs sum bcu drug nyid dang //nges pa sum bcu rtsa drug gi/lam brten bla mas lha bdud bcom//dga' bzhi sgyu ma rang bzhin med//bde chen ror 'gyur phung po bcom//
20a8(rgyud): 'bras bu rang rig ye shes sbyin//rang sems nyon mongs sdug bsngal bral//nyon mongs pa yi bdud bcom mo//de yis 'chi ba dang bral bas//de nyid 'chi bdag bdudabacom mo//'og gi lam gyi bdud bzhi yi//
20b1(rgyud): dman pa'i stobs kyis zin pa ste//rgyal po dmangs su babs pa bzhin//de nyid lam gyi bdud kyis zin//rgyal po rgyal sa shor ba bzhin//bzhi pa dang 'brel bdud bzhi ni//gsum pa'i gnod dang ldan pa la//
20b2(rgyud): bzhi pa'i lam ston bla ma ni//lha yi bu yi bdud yin te//bdud gsum bla ma'i zhal nas shes//snod min gsang bsgrogs pa dang //mar dang khar la sogs pa//'thungs te ma zhu dug 'gyur bzhin//khyad par bdud 
20b3(rgyud): kyis zin pa ste//rigs ngan rgyal sa 'dzin pa 'dra//dbang dang gdam ngag 'brel bcas pa'i//bdud 'joms lam gyi stobs dang ldan//ye shes lnga 'am yon tan drug/ldan pa dag du rab tu grags//bla ma gcig 
20b4(rgyud): la snod gcud gnyis//thob na de nyid dam pa ste//bla ma gcig la dbang thob nas//bla ma gcig la mnyan par bya//bla ma rnam pa gnyis su gsungs//phyi yi bla ma de nyid de//nang gi bla ma bdag po'i lha//bdud dang 
20b5(rgyud): dgra yi sde rnams kun//thams cad 'jigs shing skrag byed pas//'jigs pa zhes ni gsungs pa ste//dgra dang bdud kyi g.yul las rgyal//dmigs pa lha sogs bdun dang ldan//de phyir chen po zhes ni gsungs//
20b6(rgyud): de'i tshe 'khor 'dus pa de dag dang //byang chub sems dpa' rdo rje snying po dang //thams cad rgyud la dga' zhing mgu nas rjes su yid rang bar gyur to//dpal nam mkha' chen po'i rgyud kyi rgyal po rdzogs s+ho////
20b7(rgyud): rgya gar gyi mkhan po gA ya d+ha ra dang //lo ts+tsha ba dge slong shAkya ye shes kyis bsgyur ba'o////rgya gar skad du/shrI kA ya bAka tsan+ta tan tra rA dzA/bod skad du/dpal sku gsung thugs kyi rgyud kyi rgyal po//
20b8(rgyud): dpal rdo rje sems dpa' la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis 'dod chags che//mchog gyur 'di la yang dag gnas//mgon po thugs rje che ldan pa//khyod kyis bdag la bshad du gsol//rdo rje 'dzin pas 
21a1(rgyud): @#//bka' stsal ba//sku ni bum pa'i ngo bo ste//gsung ni gsang ba'i ngo bo ste//thugs kyang shes rab ye shes te//sangs rgyas spyan dang mA ma kI//dam tshig sgron ma gos dkar mo//rdo rje 'dzin la 'di skad gsol//
21a2(rgyud): rgyud kyi don la rim goms shing //smra ba'i tshig gis ma rmongs la//gzhan gyi rtsod pas mi rje yang //dbang dang man ngag ma thob par//dbang bskur rab gnas sbyin sreg sogs//sngags spyod ngal ba don med yin//shi nas sems 
21a3(rgyud): can dmyal bar skye//de phyir gtso bos dbang bskur gyi//man ngag yang dag bshad du gsol//rdo rje 'dzin pas bka' stsal pa//dang por bum dbang rim pa yang //gnas ni shin du bsngags pa btsal//bkra shis yid dga' 
21a4(rgyud): shin du snum//bkra shis bya rnams snyan par sgrogs//dpag bsam shing rgyas yod par 'dod//gzhan yang dur khrod shing gcig gam//chu ngogs dag la sogs pa ru//sa bdag sa yi lha mo la//gtor ma sbyin sreg mchod 
21a5(rgyud): yon gyis//sa bslang phyir ni tho rnams gzugs//de nas dang po bsnyen pa 'bul//bsnyen pa gsum ni rab 'bring ste//mtshan ma grangs dang dus su gsungs//re re la yang gsum gsum ste//mtshan ma gzugs dang 'od dang 
21a6(rgyud): sgra//grangs ni stong dang khri dang 'bum//dus ni bcwa lnga bcu gsum dang //bdun du bsnyen pa rdzogs pa dang //lha rnams mnyes pas bsnyen byas nas//lha dang slob ma rdzas rnams kun//lhag par gnas pa tshul bzhin bya//
21a7(rgyud): bkra shis lo grangs ldan pa yi//bu mos sa yi phyogs 'thun pa'i//ku sha btsal la mtshan ma brtag/so shing rtse mor me tog dri//bsrung skud la sogs mtshan ma brtag/dbang gi dkyil 'khor bskyed par bya//dkyil 'khor 
21a8(rgyud): slob ma rab 'bring tha//ting 'dzin me tog rdul tshon rim//gang dang ldan pa de nyid do//de nas mchod rdzas rim pa dgram//kham par yi sngags kyis kyang //de la rdo rje khyab par bya//de la ku sha la sogs pa/dkyil ba'i cho ga dang 
21b1(rgyud): bcas bya//gzhan yang sngon du 'gro ba'i rdzas//bla ma mnyes par bya ba dang //dkyil 'khor lha rnams mchod pa'i phyir//rta dang glang po ma he dang //rin chen la sogs gter rnams dang //spos dang mar me la sogs pa//brgya 'am 
21b2(rgyud): stong ngam khri 'am 'bum//bla re rgyan dang ba dan sogs//byin rlabs tshogs bsag phyi ru dgod//de nas slob ma gzhug par bya//brus dang yan lag dri yis byug/kye ho khyod ni ji lta bu//'jig rten sprul pa las sad na//he ru kar 
21b3(rgyud): grags chen po yi//dkyil 'khor chen por khyod rol lam//dad pa dang bcas spro bar gyis//rnam rtog dang bral rdo rje sems//dga' chen khyod bdag ston pa po//rdo rje 'dzin pa dgongs su gsol//byang chub chen por lung bstan pa//
21b4(rgyud): mgon po chen po bdag 'tshal lo//dam tshig de nyid bdag la stsol//byang chub sems ni bdag la bstsol//pad ma las byung bdag la stsol//gsang ba dgyes pa bdag la stsol//thar pa chen po'i grong mchog du//mgon po bdag 
21b5(rgyud): ni 'jug par 'tshal//legs so legs so rigs kyi bu//rgyal sras kun gyi nang na mchog/rnam rtog thams cad 'jigs byed pas//gnyis med khyod kyis blang bar bya//mi bskyod bcom ldan dang mtshungs pa'i//zil gyis mi 
21b6(rgyud): non khyod kyis blangs//dam tshig spyod pa spyad pa yi//mchog gi de nyid blang bar bya//thabs dang shes rab zung du sbrel//nam yang khyod kyis spang mi bya//tshangs spyod de bzhin bsam gtan la//'bad pas sems ni brtan par bya//
21b7(rgyud): chang ni rtag btung khro mi bya//de bzhin sha yang shes par bya//gzhan gyi phyag rgya bsten par bya//'di ni dam tshig brgyad yin no//dkon mchog gsum la skyabs su 'gro//thun mong sdom dang sems bskyed sbyin//bskor ba lan 
21b8(rgyud): gsum phyag gsum bya//yol bas bar du bcad nas ni//mig la sogs pa lnga bkab la//lha grangs ru yod me tog sbyin//kun byed chu yis gsang dag bya//me tog dang bcas rdo rje yang //de yi snying la gzhag la bsgom//
22a1(rgyud): @#//
22b1(rgyud): gsungs//bum pa'i dbang gi las rnams te//dkyil 'khor du 'jug dang po yin//gnyis pa rig pa'i dbang yin te//brtan par byed pa gsum pa'o//de nas gsang ba bshad bya ba//lus ni ri rab lhun po la//byang chub sems 
22b2(rgyud): kyi gnas bcas nas//bde ba'i stan la gnas nas su//gsang ba'i dbang gis rten gyur pa//rig ma dang po btsal bar bya//dang po shes pas brtag pa dang //bdag dbang yod par blang ba dang //nyes pa dag par sbyang ba dang //dkyil 
22b3(rgyud): 'khor bskyed par bya ba dang //bar chod bgegs rnams zhi ba dang //bsrung dang de la tshogs kyis mchod//dri yi thig le du ma bya//g.yon na gnas pa de nyid dang //bsrub dang bsrub bcas sbyor byas dang //srub las bde ba'i chu rgyun gyis//
22b4(rgyud): lus kun khyab nas gsang ba ru//dkar la rtsa dmar lta bur 'dzag/dkyil 'khor du 'jug gong dang 'dra//ro ma'i 'khor lor byA bsams la//lce de pad mar byin brlabs te//lce yi rtsa mor byAHbas pas la//sih+lA ga bur 'dres 
22b5(rgyud): pa yis//b+h+ya la bzhag te de la bstim//so gnyis longs spyod mnyam sbyar la//dbus su mi dmigs bsgom par bya//gsang ba'i dbang la rnam gsum ste//rdo rje rtse mor byon pa dang //sbom po khro mos sbyin pa dang //rdo rje gro tshangs sbyin 
22b6(rgyud): pa'o//shes rab ye shes dbang la yang //dang por shes rab brtag pa ni//lus dang ngag dang yid dang gsum//mkhas pas brtag cing shes par bya//de nas khyad par lnga brtags te//rigs dang lang tsho longs spyod dang //rgyan dang 
22b7(rgyud): gzugs kyi khyad par lnga//phyi yi brtag pa yin par gsungs//sems brtan rdo rje yi ni rigs//khyad par yin par nga yis gsungs//rnam rtog chung ba rdo rje yi//lang tsho'i khyad par yin par gsungs//mi rtog pas brgyan shes pa 
22b8(rgyud): ste//rdo rje'i rgyan gyi khyad par yin//de nyid gsum du mi 'gyur ba//mi nub chos kyi rgyal mtshan te//rdo rje gzugs kyi khyad par yin//chos rnams thams cad stong pa nyid//de yi ro la longs spyod pa//rdo rje'i longs spyod 
23a1(rgyud): @#//
23b1(rgyud): gnyis gtso bor bya//kha ra'i chang ngam 'o ma 'am//sha dang mnyam por sbyin par bya//'di ni yan lag spros bcas so//de nas mtshams bcad bsnyen pa dang //dam tshig sna tshogs bya ba yang //spros pa med pa'i yan lag 
23b2(rgyud): yin//dbang bskur sngon du bsgoms pa'i don//dbang thob phyir ni nga yis gsungs//rig ma gtad dang man ngag sbyin//brda rnams bya zhing 'dod pa'i rtags//brtag pa shin du spros pa med//phyag dang zhabs la sogs pas 
23b3(rgyud): 'khyud//dang po'i sbyor thabs zhes gsungs so//dbu rgyan mgul rgyan la sogs pa//sbyor thabs chen po zhes bya ste//langs pa cog bu gan rkyal dang //kha sbub 'phreng dang lnga yin te//'di rnams kun gyi dgongs pa zab//
23b4(rgyud): dgongs med spyod pa spyod byed pa//'di ni mnar med skye ba'i rgyu//tha mal pa las brgya 'gyur gyis//bum pa'i dbang dang 'brel ba'o//bum pa dang ni gsang ba ste//gsang ba las gzhan gsum pa ste//de yi rnam smin brjod 
23b5(rgyud): mi lang //dkyil 'khor du 'jug rim pa ni//bla ma'i zhal nas shes par bya//yan lag bdun bsgoms sbyor ba dngos//khyad par rnam pa gsum las byung //sngags dang sman ni dang po ste//gnyis pa sbyor thabs yin par gsungs//
23b6(rgyud): gsum pa ting nge 'dzin yin te//sngags dang sman gyis kun da drang //rked bcing tshad dang gnas pa'i tshad//grang ba'i tshad rnams shes par bya//'byung ba bzhi dang rigs 'thun pa'i//rked bcing rked pa'i tshad dang mnyam//
23b7(rgyud): sor gsum sor bzhi dang ngar ma men//gnas pa'i tshad ni rnam pa drug/rnam thar gsang ba'i pho brang gnas//skye mched rig par gnas pa'o//stong pa rta mgrin 'khor lor gnas//khams rnams gsang ba bde skyong gnas//
23b8(rgyud): 'dab la sprul pa'i 'khor lo gnas//yang dag spong ba snying khar gnas//he ru kar ni ma gshegs cing //rdo rje mda' yis ma brgyab pa'o//drang ba'i tshad ni sor bzhi 'byung //dang por khrus byas gnyis pa la//sman dang snum 
24a1(rgyud): @#//
24b1(rgyud): ma la brten nas su//bde ba'i ye shes bskyed pas na//shes rab ye shes dbang du gsungs//si h+la ga bur shes rab ste//de yi bde ba ye shes yin//si h+la shes rab zhes bya ste//ga bur ye shes su ni bshad//rtsa 
24b2(rgyud): gsum 'dus pa shes rab ste//de yi thig le ye shes yin//la lA nA ni ye shes so//rA sa nA ni shes rab ste//byang chub sems ni ye shes so//mkha' 'gro ma ni shes rab ste//dpa' bo ye shes su ni bshad//dga' ba 
24b3(rgyud): gsum ni ye shes te//rang bzhin dga' ba shes rab yin//'og ni shes rab shes bya ste//steng la ye shes su yang bshad//g.yon pa shes rab zhes bya ste//g.yas pa ye shes su yang bshad//chos dbyings shes rab ces 
24b4(rgyud): bya ste//ye shes bzhi ni ye shes te//phyag mtshan ming dang yi ge rnams//gzhan yang bye brag shes par bya//shes rab ye shes la yang gsum//tshig la bslab pa'ang gnang ba yin//gzugs brnyan la ni bshad pa ste//mngon 
24b5(rgyud): par rtogs pas bsgom pa gsungs//e ba~M yi' rnam pa bzhi//e ni sa ste ba~M ni chu//ma ni me ste yA ni rlung //e ni snying rje ba~M ni byams//ma ni dga' ba yA btang snyoms//e ni spyan ba~M mA ma kI//ma ni gos dkar yA sgrol ma//e ni 
24b6(rgyud): sbyin pa ba~M tshul khrims//ma ni bzod pa yA bsam gtan//e ni phyag brgya bzang po ste//ba~M ni dam tshig phyag rgya yin//ma ni chos kyi phyag rgya ste//yA ni shes rab phyag rgya'o//e ni shar te ba~M lho phyogs//ma ni nub phyogs yA byang 
24b7(rgyud): phyogs//e ni sku yi thig le ste//ba~M ni gsung la ma ni thugs//yA ni ye shes thig la'o//e ni sngon po ba~M ljang khu//ma ni dmar po yA ser po//e ni mkha' 'gro ba~M lA ma//ma ni dum skyes yA gzugs can//e ni ye shes mkha' 
24b8(rgyud): 'gro yin//ba~M ni rdo rje mkha' 'gro yin//ma ni sha za'i mkha' 'gro ste//yA ni las kyi mkha' 'gro'o//e ni 'gog pa ba~M ma ni lam//ma ni sdug bsngal yA kun 'byung //e ni bde chen ba~M longs spyod//ma ni chos la yA sprul pa//e ni 
25a1(rgyud): @#//mtshan la baM stong ba//ma ni rnam thar yAng pe byed//e ni rnam pa sna tshogs te//baM ni rnam par smin pa'o//ma ni rnam par nyed pa ste//yA ni rnam pa mtshan nyid bral//e ni chung ba baM 'bring po//ma ni lhag pa 
25a2(rgyud): ya cher lhag/e ni g.yas nas me rgyu ste//ba ni g.yon nas rlung rgyu'o//ma ni 'og nas sa rlung rgyu//ya ni steng nas chu rlung rgyu//e ni gnas brtan baM phal chen//ma ni bkur ba yA thams cad//e ni bsnyen pa paM nye bsnyen//ma ni bsgrub 
25a3(rgyud): pa ya bsgrub che//e ni shar lho baM lho nub//ma ni nub byang yA byang shar//e ni rgyud la baM ni sngags//ma ni ye shes yAgasang ba//e ni snang ba paM snang mched//ma ni snang thob yA 'od gsal//e ni bdag gi bzang 
25a4(rgyud): po yin//baM ni dus kyi bzang po'o//ma ni ye shes bzang por gsungs//yA ni rlung gi bzang po yin//e ni bdag gi gnas yin te//baM ni rnal 'byor gnas su bstan//ma ni gzhan gyi gnas yin te//yA ni rgyud kyi gnas yin no//
25a5(rgyud): e ni dga' ba baM mchog dga'//ma ni lhan cig yA dga' bral//e ni me long baM mnyam nyid//ma ni kun rtog yA nan tan//e ni chos sku baM longs spyod//ma ni sprul sku yA bde chen//e ni zhi ba baM ni dbang //ma ni rgyas pa yA 
25a6(rgyud): gsod pa//yi ge btus pa'i sngags tshan gsum//oM ni sku yi 'khor lo ste//AHni gsung gi 'khor lo'o//hUM ni thugs kyi 'khor lo yin//oM ni rnam snang AH'od dpag/hUM ni mi bskyod rang bzhin no//oM ni phyag g.yon 
25a7(rgyud): hUM ni g.yas//AHni dbus kyi snying ka yin//oM ni g.yon gyi spyan yin te//hUM ni g.yas pa a dbu ma//oM ni srog yin hUM rtsol ba//AHni gnas par shes par bya//oM ni sku la hUM ni thugs//A ni gsung du shes par bya//oM ni
25a8(rgyud): lus la A ni dag/hUM ni yid du shes par bya//oM n-i thabs la hUM shes rab//AHni gnyis su med pa'o//oM ni g.yon gyi la la na//hUM ni g.yas kyi rA sa na//AHni a ba d+hU tI yin//oM ni g.yas kyi ra sa na//hUM ni g.yon gyi la lA 
25b1(rgyud): na/AHni de bzhin dbus na gnas//oM ni ga bur hUM sih+la/AHni dkar la rtsa dmar yin//oM ni mchog dga' hUM khyad dga'//AHni rang bzhin dga' ba'i don//dga' ba lhan cig skyes dgar 'gyur//lhan cig skyes pa dga' 
25b2(rgyud): bar 'gyur//rgyu la 'bras bu sbyor ba dang //'bras bu la yang rgyu sbyor dang //rgyu la rgyu yi sbyor ba dang //'bras yul yang 'bras bur sbyor//rgyu 'bras gnyis su med par sbyor//thabs la shes rab sbyor ba dang //shes rab la yang thabs kyis sbyor//thabs la thabs 
25b3(rgyud): kyis sbyor ba dang //shes rabs shes rab sbyar ba dag/thabs dang shes rab gnyis med sbyor//sbyor ba 'di yi dgongs pa yang //kun mkhyen pas kyang rtogs par dka'//a ni rnam snang mi bskyod pa//na ro rin chen 'byung ldan 
25b4(rgyud): te//zla tshes 'od dpag med pa yin//thig le don yod grub pa'o//oM ni bde gshegs lnga rang bzhin//zhabs skyed don yod grub pa ste//a ni de bzhin 'od dpag med//ha ni rnam par snang mdzad de//zla tshes rin chen 'byung ldan 
25b5(rgyud): yin//thig le mi bskyod rang bzhin no//zhabs skyed sgrol ma'i rang bzhin te//a ni gos dkar ngo bo yin//ha ni rdo rje dbyings phyug ma//zla tshes ma mA kI rang bzhin//thig le spyan du shes par bya//'byung ba lnga dang sku lnga dang //'khor 
25b6(rgyud): lo lnga dang dga' ba lnga//go rims bzhin du shes par bya//zhabs skyed bya ba nan tan yin//a ni mnyam nyid ye shes so//ha ni chos kyi dbyings yin te//zla tshes so sor rtog pa'o//thig le me long ye shes so//'di dag kun 
25b7(rgyud): gyi dgongs pa ni//sangs rgyas kun gyi thugs kyi gsang //kun du gsang zhing dpyad par dka'//yi ge tshul gyis rgyas btab pa'o//de nas byang chub sems dpa' rdo rje snying po dang //mkha' 'gro ma'i tshogs kyis mchod pas 
25b8(rgyud): mchod nas mi snang bar gyur to//mkha' 'gro ma dra ba'i sdom pa sku gsung thugs kyi rgyud kyi rgyal po rdzogs s+ho////paN Di ta gA ya d+ha ra dang //lo ts+tsha ba dge slong shAkya ye shes kyis bsgyur ba'o////
26a1(rgyud): @#//rgya gar skad du/shrI rang na mA le tan tra rA dzA/bod skad du/dpal rin chen phreng ba'i rgyud kyi rgyal po//dpal bde ba chen po la phyag 'tshal lo//lha mos gsol pa rdo rje 'dzin//dpal ldan 'khor lo sdom pa ru//
26a2(rgyud): rin chen phreng ba'i don ma rtogs//khyod kyis bdag la bshad du gsol//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//nyon cig lha mo spyan bzangs ring //dpal ldan 'khor lo sdam pa ru//ma gsungs nga yis khyod la bshad//
26a3(rgyud): rin po che ni rnam pa gsum//de yi phreng ba'ang rnam pa gsum//dang po snod kyi rin po che//gnyis pa bcud kyi rin po che//snod bcud gnyis dang rang bzhin bral//de ni rin chen gsum pa yin//lha mos gsol pa bcom 
26a4(rgyud): ldan 'das//rnam pa gsum du gsungs pa las//de rnams rin chen ji ltar lags//de yi phreng ba ji ltar lags//de rnams su yi spyod yul lags//bcom ldan 'das kyis dka' stsal ba//dang po snod kyi rin bo che//rnam pa lnga ru shes par bya//ga ga ri ni 
26a5(rgyud): dkar po che//mi bskyod pa yis byin brlabs te//dbyibs ni zla gam lta bu la//rab ni dang ba'i sgo dang tsam//'di ni rin chen 'bar ba ste//pad dkar ra dkar lug mo yi//'o ma sku zhing spangs pa dang //mi bskyod pa la mchod par bya//de 
26a6(rgyud): nas spyi bor gdags pa'o//mchod pa'i bye brag kun la bya//shar gyi gdon rnams thub pa dang //gang la yod pa de la skyob//'bring ni gzhan skyes sbom po tsam//'di ni rin chen dge ba ste//dngul chu rnam pa gnyis blugs la//rkyen gyi

55b2(rgyud): pa med//sems gzung yan lag bsam mi khyab//nga yi rjes su 'brang ba yi//bla ma sa rab non pa dang //de nyid bcu ldan mngon shes skyes//de gsung bcu drug mtha' yi bar//rig pa'i gnas lnga mkhas pas spyad//mkha' 'gro 
55b3(rgyud): ma yi lung bstan pas//brtsams la bzhi yi mtha' yis nges//de lta'i man ngag ma thob par//sngags kyi lta spyod gang spyod pa//rang yang phung la gzhan yang phung //mthong ba'i chos la dmyal sogs 'tshed//de bas dngos grub 
55b4(rgyud): 'dod pa yis//bla ma dam pa mnyes byas te//man ngag dam pa blang bar bya//nges pa'i lta spyod ma rnyed bar//gsang ba'i lta dang spyod pa dang //mos pa'i rjes su gang byed dang //nyon mongs zil non la sogs dag/gsang sngags lta 
55b5(rgyud): bur spyad pa spyod//'di dag kun po ma rungs pa//mthong ba'i chos la dmya la bar skye//dang po srid pa skye ba yang //yar ngo mar ngo mtshams dang gsum//si h+la'i dang po tha ma dang //'bras bu sred pa skye ba yi//dus der 
55b6(rgyud): kun du ru byed na//'byung ba'i rlung gi nang zhugs na//nad bzhi bsdus shing nad bzhi skyed//srog dang tshangs par spyod pa 'byung //de la 'dod pa'i mtshon chas ni//g.yeng ba med dang gtar ka bya//phyi nas rigs kyi bdag 
55b7(rgyud): pos so//srid par skye ba'i dus tshod kyang //rgyun chad zhag gsum zhag lnga dang //bdun dang dgu yi dus rnams spang //de ltar rgyud mchog rgyal po'i don//khyod la gtad kyis legs par bzung //de nas 'khor thams cad

56a1(rgyud): @#//rgya gar skad du/shrI badzra rA dza ma hA tan tra/bod skad du/dpal rdo rje rgyal po chen po'i rgyud//dpal sdom pa'i rgyal po la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das 
56a2(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang /gsung dang thugs rdo rje btsun mo'i b+ha ga la rdo rje snying po la sogs pa'i dpa' bo mkha' 'gro grangs med pa dang thabs gcig tu bzhugs pa'i dus su phyi nang gsang ba bla na med pa'i 
56a3(rgyud): mchod pas mchod nas/bcom ldan 'das la mngon du phyogs pa dang /bcom ldan 'das la zhal 'dzum pa mdzad de/rdo rje snying po la sogs pas gsol pa//rdo rje gang lags chen po gang //rgyud ni gang lags rgyal po 
56a4(rgyud): gang //bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//don gyi rdo rje rnam pa bzhi//dbang bzhi'i grangs su shes par bya//rnam rtog 'joms phyir rdo rje ste//de la rnam pa bzhi yin te//phyi nang gsang ba rab gsang gnyis//rtse gsum rtse gcig rtse 
56a5(rgyud): lnga pa//rtse dgu pa ni nyid yin no//bcu gnyis de nyid rdo rje ste//rtags kyi rdo rje bzhi yin no//gnyis su med pa rtse gcig pas//rnam rtog thams cad spangs pa yin//rtse gsum sku gsung thugs yin te//'dod chags zhe sdang gti mug gsum//
56a6(rgyud): 'joms pa'i phyir na bshad pa yin//rtse lnga ye shes lnga yi rtags//me long mnyam nyid so sor rtog/bya ba nan tan chos dbyings shes//nyon mongs lnga ni spangs pa ste//zhe sdang nga rgyal 'dod chags dang //phrag dog gti mug 'joms 
56a7(rgyud): par byed//rdo rje rtse dgu gang yin pa//ye shes rtse dgu bshad pa ste//khams gsum sa dgu 'joms par byed//'dod khams gzugs khams gzugs med khams//nyan thos rang rgyal byang chub sems//rig 'dzin sangs rgyas ye shes 
56a8(rgyud): che//sa dgu la ni gang sgrib pa'i//sgrib pa sbyong bar byed pa yi//rdo rje rtse dgur bstan pa yin//rdo rje rtse mo bcu gnyis pa//'khor ba'i rten 'brel bcu gnyis kyi//spong ba'i ye shes bcu gnyis yin//ma rig pa las 'du byed de//
56b1(rgyud): 'du byed rkyen gyis rnam par shes//ming dang gzugs dang skye mched drug/reg dang tshor dang sred dang len//srid pa skye dang rga shi ste//de rnams spong ba'i gnyen po ste//chos kyi ye shes mngon du byas//de las ye shes drug rang bzhin 
56b2(rgyud): de las sku gsung thugs ngo bo//de las skye mched drug sangs rgyas//de las gnyis med rig pa ni//de las nyams myong bde chen po//rang rig de nyid sred pa ste//chos kun ston byed len pa ste//srid pa longs spyod rdzogs sku yin//
56b3(rgyud): skye ba sprul pa'i sku ru bshad//rga shi rgyun dang mya ngan 'das//'od gsal stong chen ngang du thim//de phyir rdo rje bcu gnyis pa//rdzas kyi rdo rje bzhi yin te//'bigs shing rno ba zur gsum ste//gru bzhis rdzas rnams brdar 
56b4(rgyud): byas pas//'jam zhing ngang la 'joms par byed//pha lam zlum pos rtsub bar joms//zur lngas thams cad cing gar byed//de nas chen po'i don bshad de//dmigs dang bsam pa spyod pa dang //brtul zhugs brtson 'grus smon 
56b5(rgyud): lam dang //de bzhin ye shes chen po ste//re re la yang bdun bdun sbyar//de phyir chen po zhes ni bstan//rgyud ni rnam pa lnga yin te//phyi dang nang dang gsang ba dang //rab tu gsang dang gsang ba che//de rnams rgyun du gnas pa la//rgyu yi rgyud du

57a1(rgyud): ji ltar lags//thabs gcig bzhugs pa ji ltar lags//phyi nang gsang ba bla med pa'i//mchod nas zhes pa ji ltar lags//mngon phyogs 'khod pa ji ltar lags//zhal 'dzum mdzad pa ji ltar lags//

57a4(rgyud): kun//'gro ba'i don mdzad de bzhin gshegs//thams cad dus gsum sangs rgyas kyi//sku gsung thugs kyi ngo bor bcas//btsun mo zhes bya chos kyi dbyings//de las byung phyir b+ha ga ste//dbyer mi phyed pas bzhugs par bshad//

60a8(rgyud): no//sdug pa rnam par thar pa'i lus//mngon sum byas nas rdzogs pa ni//de dag rnam thar gsum pa tsa//gzugs kyi 'du shes la 'das pa//sna tshogs 'du shes yid mi byed//nam mkha' mtha' yas la gnas
